<script type="text/javascript">
var winhref = window.location.href.replace("/content/samsung","").replace(".html","/");
if ( winhref.indexOf("?") > 0) {
	winhref = winhref.substring(0, winhref.indexOf("?"));
}

var siteCode = winhref.split("/")[3];
//cn인 경우는 경로에서 siteCode를 추출할 수 없으므로 다른 방법으로 접근
if(winhref.indexOf("samsung.com.cn") > 0) {
	siteCode = "cn";
}

//depth Info.
var depth = winhref.split("/").length;
var depth_last = winhref.split("/")[depth-1];
if(depth_last =="" || depth_last.charAt(0)=="?"){
	depth -= 1;
}

//set pathIndicator(not product page)
var pageName = "";    
var depth_2 = "";
var depth_3 = "";
var depth_4 = "";
var depth_5 = "";

var digitalData = {
	"page" : {
			"pageInfo" : {
					"siteCode"    : "vn",
					"pageName"    : pageName,
					"pageID"      : "L2NvbnRlbnQvc2Ftc3VuZy92bg==",
					"pageTrack"   : "home",
					"originPlaform" : "web"
			},
			"pathIndicator" : {
					"depth_2" : depth_2,

					"depth_5" : depth_5

	},

			<!--  script type="text/javascript" src="/etc.clientlibs/samsung/clientlibs/consumer/global/clientlib-common/resources/js/shopAppUtil.js"></script>  -->
		<script>
			let isWebView = false;
			let isPlatformReady = false;
			class ShopAppUtil {
				constructor(params) {
					this.params = params;
					// let startT = new Date().valueOf();
					// console.log("★ startTime:", startT);
					// if(!!window.flutter_inappwebview){
					let siteCode = "vn";
					let appCookie = document.cookie.match(`(^|;) ?WebView=([^;]*)(;|$)`);
					if(appCookie != null && appCookie[2] === "Y"){
						isWebView = true;
					}else if(siteCode !== "fr" && siteCode !== "in"){
						isWebView = !!window.flutter_inappwebview;
					}
					// }

					if(isWebView){
						window.addEventListener("flutterInAppWebViewPlatformReady", (event) => {
							// let responseT = new Date().valueOf();
							// console.log("★ responseTime:", responseT);
							// console.log("★ responseTime-startTime:", responseT - startT);
							// console.log("flutterInAppWebViewPlatformReady, web view:", isWebView);
							isPlatformReady = true;
							params.readyCallback();
						});
					}
				}
				callHandler = (methodName, ...params) => {
					if (isPlatformReady) {
					return window.flutter_inappwebview.callHandler(methodName, ...params)
					} else {
					return Promise.reject("Calling methodName: "+methodName+", but webview not identified")
					}
				}
				newCallHandler = (methodName, ...params) => {
					return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
						let isCall = false;
						const callHandler = () => {
							if(isCall){return;}
							isCall = true;
							//this.logger("Run: ", methodName);
							window.flutter_inappwebview.callHandler(methodName, ...params).then(resolve).catch(reject);
						}

						if(isPlatformReady){
							callHandler();
						}else{
							this.logger("Waiting: ", methodName);
							window.addEventListener("flutterInAppWebViewPlatformReady",callHandler,{once:true});
							if(isPlatformReady){
								//window.removeEventListener("flutterInAppWebViewPlatformReady", callHandler);
								callHandler();
							}
						}
					});
				}
				logger = (info, value) => {
					this.params.logger && console.log(" "+info+" "+value+" ")
				}
				isWebView = () => {
					this.logger('Returning isWebView: ', isWebView);
					return isWebView;
				}
				isPlatformReady = () => {
					this.logger('Returning isPlatformReady: ', isPlatformReady);
					return isPlatformReady;
				}
				getAppVersionCode = () => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
					this.callHandler('getAppVersionCode')
					.then(result => {
						this.logger("App version", result)
						resolve(result)
					})
					.catch(err => {
						this.logger("Error in App version", err)
						reject(err)
					})
				})
				triggerAnalytics = (data) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
					this.callHandler('OnAnalyticsEvent', data)
					.then(result => {
						this.logger("OnAnalyticsEvent Success", result)
						resolve(JSON.stringify(result))
					})
					.catch(err => {
						this.logger("Error in OnAnalyticsEvent", err)
						reject(err)
					})
				})
				openExternalBrowser = (url) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
					if (typeof url === "string") {
						url = url.trim();
					}
					this.callHandler('openExternalBrowser', url)
					.then(result => {
						this.logger("openExternalBrowser Success", result)
						resolve(JSON.stringify(result))
					})
					.catch(err => {
						this.logger("Error in openExternalBrowser", err)
						reject(err)
					})
				})
				setupCloseForBack = (exit, confirm, hide, backCallback) => {
					this.callHandler('configureBackV2', exit, confirm, hide, backCallback)
					.then(function (result) {
						console.log(JSON.stringify(result));
					})
					.catch(function (err) {
						console.log("Error in configureBackV2", err)
					})
				}
				setupNormalBack = () => {
					this.callHandler('configureBackV2', false, false, false, '')
					.then(function (result) {
						console.log(JSON.stringify(result));
					})
					.catch(function (err) {
						console.log("Error in configureBackV2", err)
					})
				}
				getUserDetails = () => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
					this.callHandler('getUserDetails', 'window.setUserDetails')
					.then(result => {
						this.logger("User Details", result)
						resolve(result)
					})
					.catch(err => {
						this.logger("Error in getUserDetails", err)
						reject(err)
					})
				})
				updateCartCount = (cartCount) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
					this.callHandler('updateCartCount', cartCount)
					.then(result => {
						this.logger("updated Cart Count", result)
						resolve(result)
					})
					.catch(err => {
						this.logger("Error in updateCartCount", err)
						reject(err)
					})
				})
				getToken = () => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
					this.callHandler('getToken', false)
					.then(result => {
						this.logger("GetToken Success", result)
						resolve(result)
					})
					.catch(err => {
						this.logger("Error in getToken", err)
						reject(err)
					})
				})
				displayInAppReview = () => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
					this.callHandler('displayInAppReview')
					.then(result => {
						this.logger("displayInAppReview success")
						resolve(result)
					})
					.catch(err => {
						this.logger("displayInAppReview failed")
						reject(err)
					})
				})
			}
			// [START log_event]
			function logEvent(name, params) {
				if (!name) {
					return;
				}

				if (window.AnalyticsWebInterface) {
					// Call Android interface
					window.AnalyticsWebInterface.logEvent(name, JSON.stringify(params));
				} else if (window.webkit
					&& window.webkit.messageHandlers
					&& window.webkit.messageHandlers.firebase) {
					// Call iOS interface
					var message = {
						command: 'logEvent',
						name: name,
						parameters: params
					};
					window.webkit.messageHandlers.firebase.postMessage(message);
				} else {
					// No Android or iOS interface found
					console.log("No native APIs found.");
				}
			}
			// [END log_event]

			// [START set_user_property]
			function setUserProperty(name, value) {
				if (!name || !value) {
					return;
				}

				if (window.AnalyticsWebInterface) {
					// Call Android interface
					window.AnalyticsWebInterface.setUserProperty(name, value);
				} else if (window.webkit
					&& window.webkit.messageHandlers
					&& window.webkit.messageHandlers.firebase) {
					// Call iOS interface
					var message = {
						command: 'setUserProperty',
						name: name,
						value: value
					};
					window.webkit.messageHandlers.firebase.postMessage(message);
				} else {
					// No Android or iOS interface found
					console.log("No native APIs found.");
				}
			}
			// [END set_user_property]

			/*
			document.getElementById("event1").addEventListener("click", function() {
			    console.log("event1");
			    logEvent("event1", { foo: "bar", baz: 123 });
			});

			document.getElementById("event2").addEventListener("click", function() {
			  console.log("event2");
			    logEvent("event2", { size: 123.456 });
			});

			document.getElementById("userprop").addEventListener("click", function() {
			    console.log("userprop");
			    setUserProperty("userprop", "custom_value");
			});
			*/

		</script>
		<script>
			const hideHeaderFooterByWindowFlutterInappwebview = () => {
				//$('.gnb').hide();
				if(document.querySelector(".gnb") != null && document.querySelector(".gnb").style != null) {
					document.querySelector(".gnb").style.display='none';
				}
				if(document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb") != null && document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb").style != null) {
					document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb").style.display='none';
				}
				if(document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb-v3") != null && document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb-v3").style != null) {
					document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb-v3").style.display='none';
				}
				if(document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb-v4") != null && document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb-v4").style != null) {
					document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb-v4").style.display='none';
				}
				if(document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb-v5") != null && document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb-v5").style != null) {
					document.querySelector(".nv00-gnb-v5").style.display='none';
				}
				if(document.querySelector(".nv07-explore-floating-navigation") != null) {

						if (!!href && href.indexOf("javascript") === -1 && href !== "#") {
							if (href.startsWith("/" + "vn" + "/")) {
								href = window.location.origin + href;
							}
							if (href.indexOf("http:") === -1 && href.indexOf("https:") === -1) {
								href = "https://" + href;

							modelCode = BC_MODEL.modelCode;
							familyCode = BC_PRODUCTS[modelCode]?.familyCode;
							break;
						case "page-wearable-bc":
						case "page-wearable-bc-v2":
							modelCode = document.getElementById("modelCode").value;
							familyCode = document.getElementById("fmyId").value;
							break;
						default:
					}
					if(modelCode){
						let currentDate = new Date();
						currentDate = currentDate.getFullYear() + "-" + String(currentDate.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(currentDate.getDate()).padStart(2, "0") + " " + String(currentDate.getHours()).padStart(2, "0") + ":" + String(currentDate.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0") + ":" + String(currentDate.getSeconds()).padStart(2, "0");

						shopAppUtilInstance.newCallHandler("addRecentlyViewed", {
							familyCode: familyCode,
							modelCode: modelCode,
							updated: currentDate
						});
					}
				}
			});

	<script type="text/javascript">
	/**
	 * [설정] 상수 및 구성
	 */
	const URL_HISTORY_KEY = 'URL_History';
	const MAX_HISTORY_COUNT = 20; // 최대 보관할 기록 개수
	
	/**
	 * 로컬 스토리지 URL 히스토리 저장 (인코딩 URL + 10자리 타임스탬프)
	 * @param {string} url - 저장할 페이지 URL
	 */
	//갯수 제한용
	function appendUrlHistoryWithTime(url) {
	    // 1. 데이터 가공: URL 인코딩 및 10자리 타임스탬프 생성
	    const encodedUrl = encodeURIComponent(url);
	    const timestamp = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000); // 13자리 밀리초를 10자리 초 단위로 변환
	    const newDataUnit = encodedUrl + '~' + timestamp; // URL과 시간을 ~로 구분하여 결합
	    
	    // 2. 기존 데이터 가져오기 및 배열로 변환
	    let existingValue = localStorage.getItem(URL_HISTORY_KEY);
	    //기존 데이터 조회
	    //console.log(`URL_HISTORY_KEY before existingValue: ` + existingValue);
	    let historyArray = [];
	    
	    if (existingValue) {
	        historyArray = existingValue.split('|'); // 기존 데이터를 배열로 분할
	    }
	    
	    // 3. 새로운 기록을 배열 끝에 추가
	    historyArray.push(newDataUnit);
	    //히스토리 사이즈 조회
	    //console.log(`URL_HISTORY_KEY historyArray.length: ` + historyArray.length); 
	    
	    // 4. FIFO 방식으로 최대 개수 제한 (오래된 것부터 제거)
	    while (historyArray.length > MAX_HISTORY_COUNT) {
	        historyArray.shift(); // 배열의 첫 번째 요소(가장 오래된 기록) 제거
	    }
	    
	    // 5. 배열을 다시 문자열로 변환하여 저장
	    const updatedValue = historyArray.join('|');
	    
	    // 6. 최종 저장 (에러 처리 포함)
	    try {
	        localStorage.setItem(URL_HISTORY_KEY, updatedValue);
	        //console.log(`[`+URL_HISTORY_KEY+`] 업데이트 완료 (길이: `+updatedValue.length+`)`);
	        //console.log(`URL_HISTORY_KEY after existingValue: ` + localStorage.getItem(URL_HISTORY_KEY));//데이터 조회
	    } catch (e) {
	        console.error("Storage Error:", e);
	        // 저장 실패 시 더 오래된 데이터를 제거하여 재시도
	        if (historyArray.length > 1) {
	            historyArray.shift(); // 하나 더 제거
	            try {
	                localStorage.setItem(URL_HISTORY_KEY, historyArray.join('|'));
	            } catch (retryError) {
	                console.error("URL_HISTORY_KEY Retry Storage Error:", retryError);
	            }
	        }
	    }
	}//:~ function appendUrlHistoryWithTime(url) 

	// 히스토리 조회 함수
	function getUrlHistory() {
	    const historyData = localStorage.getItem(URL_HISTORY_KEY);
	    if (!historyData) return [];
	    
	    return historyData.split('|').map(item => {
	        const [encodedUrl, timestamp] = item.split('~');
	        return {
	            url: decodeURIComponent(encodedUrl),
	            timestamp: parseInt(timestamp, 10),
	            date: new Date(parseInt(timestamp, 10) * 1000)
	        };
	    });
	}

	// 히스토리 초기화 함수
	function clearUrlHistory() {
	    localStorage.removeItem(URL_HISTORY_KEY);
	}
	
	//====================================================================================
	// 기존 코드 최소 수정
	function executeUrlHistoryTracking() {
	    try {
	        //갯수 
	        appendUrlHistoryWithTime(window.location.href);
	        //NOTICE : 확인 해야 할때 주석 제거
	        //console.log("URL_HISTORY_KEY getUrlHistory():", getUrlHistory());
	    } catch (error) {
	        console.error('URL History tracking error:', error);
	    }
	}

	// 초기 로드 (기존 로직 유지하되 중복 실행 방지)
	let hasExecutedInitialLoad = false;

	if (document.readyState === 'complete') {
	    executeUrlHistoryTracking();
	    hasExecutedInitialLoad = true;
	} else {
	    window.addEventListener('load', () => {
	        //위 상태에서 실행되지 않은 경우에만 실행 
	        if (!hasExecutedInitialLoad) {
	            executeUrlHistoryTracking();
	            hasExecutedInitialLoad = true;
	        }
	    });
	}

	// Route Change 감지 추가
	let currentUrl = window.location.href;

	// SPA 라우트 변경 감지(뒤로가기 또는 앞으로가기 시 발생) 필요시 hasExecutedInitialLoad 관련 추가
	['popstate'].forEach(eventType => {
	    window.addEventListener(eventType, () => {
	        if (currentUrl !== window.location.href) {
	            currentUrl = window.location.href;
	            setTimeout(executeUrlHistoryTracking, 100);
	        }
	    });
	});

	// pushState/replaceState 감지(URL이 변경될때)
	const originalPushState = history.pushState;          // 1. 기존의 원본 pushState 함수를 변수에 저장해 둡니다. (나중에 호출하기 위함)
	history.pushState = function(...args) {               // 2. history.pushState를 새로운 함수로 덮어씌웁니다 (Monkey Patching).
	    originalPushState.apply(this, args);              // 3. 원본 pushState 기능을 그대로 수행하여 URL을 실제로 변경합니다.
	    if (currentUrl !== window.location.href) {        // 4. URL 변경 후, 실제로 URL이 바뀌었는지 확인합니다.
	        currentUrl = window.location.href;                  // 새로운 URL 저장
	        setTimeout(executeUrlHistoryTracking, 100);   // 5. 0.1초(100ms) 후에 executeUrlHistoryTracking 함수를 실행합니다.
	    }
	};
	</script>
